[Content of 2026_10_01_STEA_004_ACMS_LLM_Manager_A2A_Production_Path_Implementation.md]:
# STEA-004 Entrepreneur Execution Plan
## Production Jira -> ACMS -> A2A -> Hermes Work Delivery, Durable Worker Pool, Local-Model Preference, Live Agent Streaming, Human Inbox, Projects/Products, Artifacts, and Cost

**Date:** 2026-10-01  
**Executor:** STEA-004 Entrepreneur  
**Scope:** ACMS + LLM Manager / Server Manager + Agent Runtime Manager / Agent Bridge + Hermes workers + Jira + Homarr/Service Registry + supporting telemetry  
**Goal:** Build the real production path. The final live Jira demo is an acceptance test, not a demo-only shortcut.

---

# 0. Bottom Line

The current ACMS is not yet allowed to claim that a worker is actively doing work merely because a Work Item was assigned.

Current blocking gap:

```text
Jira / ACMS Work Item
    -> assignment recorded
    -> NO real A2A delivery to acms-worker-001
    -> worker does not actually execute
```

Build the real path:

```text
Jira
  -> ACMS work intake + project/product context
  -> worker scheduler
  -> A2A assignment envelope
  -> Agent Bridge on selected worker
  -> Hermes session
  -> LLM Manager
  -> preferred local model (Qwen3.8-Flash-Next first)
  -> live stream + heartbeat + usage + artifacts
  -> completion callback
  -> ACMS result/handoff
  -> short Jira summary + ACMS-hosted artifact link
```

Do not implement a fake demo path.

---

# 1. Human Decisions - Treat as Approved Requirements

## 1.1 Worker placement

- Move `acms-worker-001` from `MIAM00111` to `MIAM00100`.
- Make `MIAM00100` the default Proxmox host for general AI-agent workers.
- Worker/agent identity must be durable across:
  - Proxmox live migration;
  - cold migration;
  - VM recreation;
  - node failure;
  - runtime restart.
- Identity must live in ACMS/Agent Bridge configuration, **not** be inferred from:
  - VMID;
  - VM hostname;
  - Proxmox node;
  - IP address.

### Hard placement exclusions

Never place general AI-agent worker VMs on:

```text
MIAM00133
MIAM00147
```

These are reserved for critical system services / backup responsibilities.

### Inference-server worker limit

Inference servers may host at most:

```text
1 AI worker runtime per inference server
```

Use an inference node only when:

- MIAM00100 capacity is not appropriate/available; or
- deliberate locality to a model offers a useful reason;
- placing the worker does not materially harm inference capacity.

If an inference-host worker is already busy, prefer an available worker on MIAM00100.

### Capacity overflow

When MIAM00100 reaches its configured worker-capacity threshold:

1. choose another eligible Proxmox node;
2. exclude MIAM00133 and MIAM00147;
3. strongly de-prioritize inference hosts;
4. enforce max 1 worker on each inference node;
5. use measured CPU/RAM/storage pressure rather than node-name guesses.

---

# 2. Worker / Agent Identity Model

Use a generic worker pool with durable worker identities.

```text
ACMS Agent Identity
    durable
    e.g. acms-worker-001
    stable UUID
    model preference
    capabilities
    trust class

Runtime Instance
    movable/recreatable
    VM/container/process
    current Proxmox node
    VMID
    current IP
    current boot/session id

Work Item
    business task
    belongs to exactly one Project
    optionally related to a Product

Execution Session
    one attempt by one worker on one Work Item
```

Do not bind durable ACMS identity to VM124 or MIAM00111.

VM124 may remain the current runtime identifier during migration, but ACMS must treat runtime location as mutable metadata.

---

# 3. One Task Per Worker

A worker may have:

```text
0 or 1 active Work Item
```

Never assign a second Work Item until the first is:

```text
SUCCEEDED
FAILED
CANCELED
DEFERRED / safely paused
```

If a worker receives an assignment while busy:

- reject with deterministic `409 WORKER_BUSY`;
- include current Work Item ID and Execution Session ID;
- scheduler chooses another idle worker;
- do not queue invisible work inside the worker.

Priority is scheduler metadata, not permission to multitask one worker.

---

# 4. Work State Model

Remove `ELIGIBLE` as a human-facing Work Item state.

Eligibility may remain an internal boolean/predicate:

```text
jira_gate_open = true/false
```

Recommended ACMS execution states:

```text
PLANNED
DISPATCHING
RUNNING
BLOCKED
HUMAN_ATTENTION
DEFERRED
IN_REVIEW
SUCCEEDED
FAILED
CANCELED
```

Rules:

- `PLANNED`: work exists; not actively running.
- `DISPATCHING`: short-lived transport state while waiting for worker ACK.
- `RUNNING`: worker ACKed assignment and Hermes execution started.
- `BLOCKED`: external dependency prevents execution.
- `HUMAN_ATTENTION`: agent needs a human decision/input.
- `DEFERRED`: intentionally postponed; context preserved.
- `IN_REVIEW`: output exists and awaits human review.
- `SUCCEEDED`: technical execution completed. Does not auto-complete Jira.
- `FAILED`: terminal failure for this execution attempt.
- `CANCELED`: intentionally canceled.

Do not show `ACTIVE` unless it means actual `RUNNING`.

---

# 5. Jira Workflow - Current Authoritative Interpretation

Use the current unified Jira workflow:

```text
IDEA / UNVALIDATED
    -> TO START
    -> IN PROGRESS
    -> IN REVIEW
    -> COMPLETED
```

Exception paths:

```text
BLOCKED
DEFERRED
```

Important:

- Older Kanban documentation uses `COMMITTED`.
- The current process uses `IN PROGRESS`.
- Treat `COMMITTED` in the older SOP as stale/legacy terminology.
- Do **not** reintroduce a `Committed` Jira state.
- Do not change the Jira workflow during this implementation.
- Reporter controls Blocked/Deferred decisions in the human process.

---

# 6. Jira Is the Human Kickoff / Governance Gate

The ACMS must reconcile Jira status downstream.

For a Jira issue assigned to:

```text
startupteamscompany@gmail.com
```

use Jira state as a control input.

### IDEA / UNVALIDATED

```text
ACMS Work = PLANNED
execution permitted = NO
```

### TO START

```text
execution permitted = YES
ACMS may dispatch
```

This is the AI kickoff gate.

### IN PROGRESS

```text
execution permitted = YES
continue execution
```

### BLOCKED

```text
do not begin new execution
if running:
    safe pause / steer / stop according to capability
create/update Human Inbox if human action is required
```

### DEFERRED

```text
do not execute
stop/pause safely
preserve work context
preserve session/artifacts
```

### IN REVIEW

```text
do not start a new implementation attempt automatically
preserve completed output
wait for reviewer
```

### COMPLETED

```text
terminal human-approved state
no new execution
```

Every Jira reconcile must compare current Jira state with ACMS state.

Examples:

- Jira changes to Deferred:
  - ACMS must stop/pause downstream work.
  - Do not continue execution because an older assignment existed.
- Jira changes to Blocked:
  - ACMS must stop or steer the running session into a safe waiting state.
- Jira changes back to To Start:
  - ACMS may resume or create a new execution session based on whether the old session is resumable.

# 9. Project / Product / Sprint / Tag Data Model

## Product

Product = business/customer offering.

Examples:

```text
AgentifyMe.co Speed-to-Lead
future customer products
other Startup Teams products
```

Product owns durable business context:

```text
business concept
customer/persona context
product goals
product-level decisions
related projects
related sprints
related Jira work
default model policy where useful
tags
```

## Project

Project = bounded implementation/context area.

Examples:

```text
ACMS
LLM Manager
PDU Manager
MARION Infrastructure
Speed-to-Lead Gateway
Customer Deployment X
```

Rules:

- Every ACMS Work Item MUST belong to exactly one Project.
- A Project MAY belong to one Product.
- Infrastructure Projects do not need a Product.
- PDU Manager is a good example: Project yes; Product optional/none.

## Sprint

Sprint is execution cadence/context.

- Sprints may contain work from more than one Project.
- Do not use Sprint as a substitute for Project.
- Link Sprint as separate metadata.

## Tags

Import useful Jira context into tags:

```text
Jira labels
Jira project key
Sprint
components
repo
customer
product
project
department
technical domain
```

Tags are additive semantic context, not the primary relational model.

Preferred long-term solution:

```text
Jira custom field: ACMS Product
Jira custom field: ACMS Project
```

If custom fields are not practical immediately:

- infer Project from known Jira project/repository mapping;
- use labels as explicit fallback.

Recognize machine-safe labels such as:

Do not confuse `acms-create-sprint` with creating a Product or Project.

If Product/Project fields are missing:

1. use deterministic mapping rules;
2. show mapping confidence;
3. allow human correction in ACMS;
4. never silently attach work to an unrelated Product.

# 12. A2A Assignment Delivery - Critical Missing Feature

Existing ACMS message says:

```text
Recorded assignment only - delivery to the agent (A2A) is not implemented yet
```

That must disappear after implementation.

## Delivery contract

ACMS sends one assignment envelope to Agent Bridge.

Required fields:

```json
{
  "schema_version": "acms-a2a-assignment-v1",
  "assignment_id": "...",
  "idempotency_key": "...",
  "agent_id": "...",
  "work_item_id": "...",
  "execution_session_id": "...",
  "jira_issue_key": "...",
  "project": {"id": "...", "name": "..."},
  "product": {"id": "...", "name": "..."},
  "sprint": {"id": "...", "name": "..."},
  "tags": [],
  "instructions": "...",
  "completion_condition": "...",
  "output_artifacts_expected": [],
  "model_policy": {},
  "priority": "...",
  "callback_urls": {
    "heartbeat": "...",
    "events": "...",
    "completion": "...",
    "artifacts": "...",
    "human_attention": "..."
  },
  "created_at": "..."
}
```

`product` and `sprint` may be null.

`project` may not be null.

## ACK contract

Example:

```json
{
  "accepted": true,
  "agent_id": "...",
  "work_item_id": "...",
  "execution_session_id": "...",
  "runtime_instance_id": "...",
  "accepted_at": "...",
  "effective_model_policy": {}
}
```

Only after ACK + Hermes execution start may ACMS mark `RUNNING`.

## Busy response

If worker has active Work Item:

```http
409 WORKER_BUSY
```

Return current Work Item ID and Execution Session ID.

## Delivery semantics

Use:

```text
at-least-once transport
+
idempotent assignment handling
```

Assignment ID + idempotency key must prevent duplicate Hermes runs.

## Retry

Use bounded retry/backoff.

If no worker ACKs:

```text
DISPATCHING -> BLOCKED or PLANNED
```

Create a Human Inbox item if intervention is needed.

Do not leave a phantom active item.

# 19. Local Model Failover

Preferred first model:

```text
qwen3.8-flash-next
```

Verify production route live through LLM Manager before use.

Do not hardcode stale backend data without checking.

## Failover policy

Attempt 1: preferred local model.

If:

```text
hard error
unhealthy route
or no first token within 10 minutes
```

then:

1. cancel/terminate hanging inference safely;
2. choose next suitable healthy local model;
3. record failover event and reason.

Attempt 2: alternate suitable local model.

If that also hard fails or TTFT exceeds 10 minutes, cloud fallback may occur **only if policy allows it**.

Cloud fallback must:

- use LLM Manager;
- use constrained cloud fallback credential;
- obey spending limits;
- log provider/model/cost;
- be visible in ACMS;
- never pretend it was local.

For `local-only`: never cloud.

---

- verify LLM Manager reports local physical attribution;
- verify chat request;

- verify worker calls LLM Manager, not OpenRouter directly.

```text
Host: <live verified host>
```

# 22. MIAM00100 Migration of acms-worker-001

This plan explicitly authorizes migration.

Before migration:

1. prove VM124 identity/config;
2. prove current Agent Bridge config;
3. back up configuration;
4. capture ACMS agent UUID;
5. capture current VM config;
6. capture IP/network state;
7. verify MIAM00100 capacity;
8. verify migration path/storage compatibility.

Then:

- stop active work first;
- no Work Item may be RUNNING during migration;
- migrate VM124 to MIAM00100 if safe;
- otherwise recreate runtime on MIAM00100 using same durable Agent identity;
- preserve agent credentials/config securely;
- start;
- verify heartbeat;
- verify A2A;
- verify model access;
- update runtime location metadata automatically.

Acceptance:

```text
Agent identity unchanged
Proxmox node changed
ACMS sees same durable agent
A2A works
heartbeat works
no work history lost
```

Manual Proxmox migration later must not require creating a new ACMS agent.

(1) Self-\n  hosted runners REUSE RUNNER_TEMP — stale `llm-manager-*.tar.gz` survive jobs\n  and `find | head -1` picks them: always rm stale tarballs + select by EXACT\n  name (short-sha form — see the release-transaction section). (2) Do NOT `rm\n  artifact.zip` in the unpack step — the download step writes it fresh; rm-ing\n  it after download kills unzip (\"cannot find or open artifact.zip\"). (3)\n  `dist/` was TRACKED in the repo (no .gitignore entry) — the CI artifact zip\n  contained multiple committed stale tarballs; fixed by git-rm + gitignore +\n  a CI step asserting exactly ONE tarball pair before upload.\n  Verify staging health: `curl -sk https://10.0.20.131/healthz` must report\n  the current main git_sha — a \"successful\" deploy-staging run proves nothing\n  on its own.\n\n## PVE qga exec pattern (how you touch VM114/VM124)\n\n- Helper: `~/work/pve_api.py` (LLDAP bot account, `~/.pve_ldap_bot`). Node\n  locations: **VM114 + VM120 + CT122 on `miam-00135`**; **VM124 on\n  `miam00111`**; VM156 on `miam-00133`. Wrong node → 500 \"qemu-server/N.conf\n  does not exist\".\n- Two-step: `POST /nodes/{node}/qemu/{vmid}/agent/exec` (returns pid) → sleep\n  → `GET .../agent/exec-status?pid=<pid>` (out-data/err-data/exitcode).\n- Stage scripts as base64 (≤9KB chunks) written to a temp file, then decoded\n  and run. VM114 qga has a wedge risk on long execs — keep each exec\n  minimal, use nohup + log-poll for long operations.\n- **VM114 has root SSH too** (`ssh vm114` alias via `~/.ssh/config.d/`, dedicated\n  key) — simpler than qga for file staging (`scp` tarballs/probes to /tmp) and\n  log polling. Deploy-flow exceptions where qga/node-SSH is still required:\n  see `vm114-qga-ssh-recovery` skill.\n\n## ARM reconciler semantics (services/reconciliation.py)\n\n- desired RUNNING: observe → start owned VM once (ownership-verified, bounded\n  retries w/ capped backoff, `recovery_count`) → RUNNING; PVE truth corrects\n  stale DB actuals.\n- desired STOPPED: graceful shutdown once → **bounded convergence wait**\n  (`ARM_RECONCILE_SHUTDOWN_WAIT_S`, default 30s/3s polls). Live lesson: PVE\n  state flips LAG the API ack — an immediate status read still says `running`\n  after an accepted shutdown. Never record nonconvergence without the wait.\n- DESIRED_DESTROYED is API-only; the reconciler NEVER destroys. PVE\n  unreachable → record `last_error`, no state flip on unknown ground truth.\n- Single-replica discipline (TDR-0009): one uvicorn worker; no leader election\n  yet. state-sync health vocabulary: PENDING|SYNCING|VERIFIED|STALE|FAILED.\n- Stale failed-provisioning rows: disposition is **SUPERSEDED** (never delete).\n  Admin endpoint marks each failed attempt row SUPERSEDED and links it to the\n  live runtime (5 ERROR rows → live VM124 runtime 56c1b849, 2026-09-28, PR\n  #50). Needs a PG enum migration for the new actual-state value; verify via\n  DB audit-event count, and `expire_all()` after API calls in tests (session\n  caches the pre-update row) — a 404-before-422 test ordering trap means the\n  bad-target test needs an existing row to hit 422.\n- **Recovery policy (PR #51, 2026-09-29, human direction):** default\n  `max_recovery_attempts` is now **5** (env `ARM_RECONCILE_MAX_ATTEMPTS`\n  overrides; prod has NO env override → 5 active). Backoff 10/20/40/60s capped\n  (attempts vary). Every attempt audits `failure_class` /\n  `recovery_method` / `attempt` in the RuntimeEvent detail (durable audit log\n  IS the recovery-method history — no second mutable table). Exhaustion\n  audits `strategy_next: human_attention`. Ladder: recheck/observe → VM start\n  ×5 → Human Attention. Service/harness restarts belong to LLM Manager\n  serving / ACMS bridge, NOT ARM; PDU hard-cycle is NEVER automatic. Tests:\n  `tests/server_manager/test_recovery_policy.py` (shared fakes imported from\n  test_reconciliation — import `make_runtime` too or NameError).\n\n## LLM Manager web UI (window-5 additions — /admin/fleet, /admin/agents)\n\n- **Fleet one-card bug (fixed 2026-09-30, PR #69, prod 439cf8d):** `_physical_host_for_ip()` led with\n  `ip.startswith(\"10.0.20.16\")` which matched EVERY guest .161-.168 → all hosts collapsed onto\n  MIAM-00111 → `_active_hosts()` promoted only VM102 → the dashboard rendered ONE card while 5 served.\n  Second stacked bug: the demotion loop checked `if phys not in promoted` and silently DROPPED a rollback\n  VM sharing the active one's physical host (docstring claimed \"demoted to the END\"). Fix: exact-IP checks\n  first; demote-but-never-drop via an active_ips set. Lesson: **IP→group mappers must lead with exact\n  matches, every \"demote\" path must append, and a test must count distinct groups over the real\n  inventory** (`tests/test_fleet_visibility.py`). Verify such bugs by importing the DEPLOYED module on prod\n  and calling the function — code reading kept saying \"fine\".\n- **Model Fleet page (PR #70, prod 81ca2b3):** `GET /api/fleet` + `/admin/fleet` — inventory-driven (hosts\n  table ∪ legacy VLLM_HOSTS; nothing hard-coded in the UI), operator states SERVING / ONLINE_IDLE /\n  STOPPED_EXPECTED / UNREACHABLE / MISCONFIGURED / STALE (routable model last_verified > 48h; aliases\n  don't mask staleness). Powered-off/unreachable hosts stay visible; \"VM powered on\" and \"model healthy\"\n  are separate facts; drill-down per host; PDU hard-power never on this page.\n- **Agent Runtimes UI (PR #71, prod d021f70):** `/admin/agents` list + `/admin/agents/{id}` detail +\n  `/admin/agents/new` wizard in the operator shell (LDAP session). The web app proxies the SM/ARM API\n  (:8300) server-side via the `svc-server-manager` identity from the same 0600 service_tokens file (same\n  VM; token never echoed). Wizard = 6 steps → the EXISTING ARM provisioning path (authority fixed\n  server-side `sprint_execution_context`); external/customer baseline shown as NOT authorized; budget\n  fields point at ACMS (never faked); destroy NOT exposed. Failure classes render actionable errors\n  (503 no-token / 599 network / upstream status) — never raw 500s.\n- **New-app dependency rule:** anything imported at module level of the web app must be in\n  **[project].dependencies** (base), not only `[project.optional-dependencies].dev` — the Dockerfile\n  installs `pip install .` (base only). Hit live: `httpx` was dev-only, so `jira_client` (imported by\n  window-5 gate code) would 500 in prod; PR moved httpx to base deps. Audit imports-vs-deps before\n  releasing any new integration module.\n\n- Slice 1 done (hosts/active_hosts/model_registry reflective models + read\n  endpoints). Full 22-table inventory in the 2026-09-28 flight handoff.\n  LiteLLM_* tables are NEVER \"migrated\" (adapter boundary).\n- **Slice 5+6 done + CUTOVER LIVE (PRs #60/#66, 2026-09-29):** the v011 legacy\n  app routes hosts/model_registry/recovery_events + gpu_samples family writes\n  through sync SQLAlchemy Core adapters behind `ORM_WRITE_CUTOVER=1`\n  (systemd drop-ins on web+recovery+collector; default OFF = legacy psycopg2;\n  rollback = remove drop-ins + daemon-reload + restart).\n  - `service/app/orm_write_adapter.py` — sync Core over the SAME schema (no\n    DDL, column whitelist, v011 credential contract: LLM_MANAGER_PG_HOST env\n    fallback + PG_PW_FILE `PG_PW=` line; prod DB is CT115 @ 10.0.20.116, NOT\n    127.0.0.1). v011_core desired-state/power-off/sync_registry/routable_models\n    + v011_recovery audit()/recent_count() call it.\n  - `service/app/gpu_orm.py` — BULK insert (one multi-row INSERT per collector\n    cycle, never row-at-a-time), hourly_rollup_window (identical upsert SQL\n    incl. power_sum_wh/last_ts + DO UPDATE refresh), bounded prune on\n    `sample_id` PK (gpu_samples has NO `id` column), parity checksums.\n  - **Legacy-parity finding:** `model_registry` UNIQUE(logical_model_name,\n    host_id) does NOT dedupe NULL host_ids (PG treats NULLs as distinct) —\n    alias inserts need the legacy delete-then-insert order or they duplicate.\n  - **cmd_rollup window MUST be hour-aligned at the START edge** (W2's\n    backfill-chunk lesson applies to every rolling window): `now-3h` mid-hour\n    truncated the oldest bucket and later runs never re-covered it → permanent\n    deficit (verify FAIL 104 mismatches, rollup 134 vs raw 142). Fix + live\n    repair (re-aggregate 24h → 576 buckets) → VERIFY PASSED 0 failures.\n  - `sqlalchemy` was in the requirements freeze but NOT in the prod venv —\n    pip-installed 2.0.54 into `/opt/llm-manager/venv` (venv is NOT rebuilt by\n    the release transaction; freeze drift is now known debt).\n  - **The systemd unit's WorkingDirectory (/opt/llm-manager/app) is a STALE\n    static dir; the RUNNING process cwd is\n    `/opt/llm-manager/releases/<sha>/service/app`.** Scripts probing deployed\n    code must `sys.path.insert(0, \"/opt/llm-manager/current/app\")` — and\n    `current/app` is a symlink into the active release.\n  - ORM is NOT imported by the FastAPI app for these domains yet — the flag\n    gates the legacy call sites themselves; parity tests live in\n    tests/test_orm_slice5_cutover.py + tests/test_gpu_orm_slice6.py (pgserver;\n    set PG_HOST=<socket dir>, PG_USER=postgres, PG_PW=\"\" empty-string VALID —\n    `if pw is None` not `if not pw`, or empty trust passwords break).\n- Slice 4 done (PR #52): read-only reflective models for\n  `recovery_events / request_routing_log / electricity_rates / manager_settings`\n  (`server_manager/llm_manager/models/orm_slice4.py`), schema mapped from\n  prod via `information_schema` (verify live columns before writing a model —\n  dates are DATE not String, PKs vary). {"success": true, "name": "jira-sprint-task-writing", "description": "Write Jira sprint tasks in the AgentifyMe format: role-based, numbered, with action verbs, specific deliverables, constraints, double-checks, and PM exit criteria.", "tags": ["entrepreneurship", "sprint-planning", "jira", "project-management", "agentifyme"], "related_skills": ["business-idea-systems"], "content": "---\nname: jira-sprint-task-writing\ndescription: \"Write Jira sprint tasks in the AgentifyMe format: role-based, numbered, with action verbs, specific deliverables, constraints, double-checks, and PM exit criteria.\"\nversion: 1.0.0\nauthor: c01entrepreneur_bot\nlicense: MIT\nmetadata:\n  hermes:\n    tags: [entrepreneurship, sprint-planning, jira, project-management, agentifyme]\n    related_skills: [business-idea-systems]\n---\n\n# Jira Sprint Task Writing\n\nUse this skill when asked to create sprint tasks, Jira issues, or engineering work plans for StartupTeams business models. The format is modeled on the AgentifyMe sprint pattern.\n\n## When to use\n\n- An entrepreneur or PM asks for sprint tasks for a business model\n- You need to convert an investor-reviewed business model into actionable engineering work\n- You need to push tasks to Jira via the Zapier MCP integration\n- You need to create a sprint plan that multiple roles (engineer, sales, PM) can execute\n\n## Process\n\n### 1. Angel investor review\n\nRoleplay a skeptical angel investor reviewing the business model. Evaluate:\n- Is the pain real and frequent?\n- Is the buyer defined and reachable?\n- Is there a defensible moat?\n- Is this achievable by a SaaS team (software + AI, no physical products)?\n- What is the weakest assumption?\n\nOutput a clear verdict: FUND, FUND WITH CHANGES, PARK, or KILL.\n\n### 2. Entrepreneur update\n\nBased on investor feedback, update the business model:\n- Narrow the persona if too broad\n- Pivot the wedge if the original doesn't hold\n- Update pricing, assumptions, and competitive moat\n- Mark PARKED models explicitly with reason\n\n### 3. Role handoffs\n\nWrite specific handoff instructions for:\n- **Senior Systems Engineer**: What to build, what NOT to build, tech stack constraints\n- **Senior Sales**: Target buyer, outreach channels, discovery questions, success criteria\n\n### 4. PM sprint tasks\n\nWrite sprint tasks in this exact format:\n\n```\n## Sprint N: [Goal in one sentence]\n\nSprint goal: [What must be true when this sprint ends]\nSprint duration: [N weeks]\n\n### [Role Name]\n\n1. [Action verb] [specific deliverable] with [constraints].\n2. [Action verb] [specific deliverable] with [constraints].\n\n### [Senior Role] double checks\n\n1. [Verify] before [gate condition].\n2. [Verify] before [gate condition].\n\n### Senior Project Manager advice\n\n1. [Exit criteria or focus constraint].\n2. [Risk assignment or definition-of-done].\n```\n\n## Writing rules\n\n1. **Action verbs only**: Build, Add, Implement, Document, Benchmark, Create, Deploy, Test, Prepare, Collect, Write, Confirm, Schedule, Post, Draft.\n2. **Specific deliverables**: Not \"improve security\" but \"Add read-only status cards for Proxmox, Tailscale, and uptime checks.\"\n3. **Constraints in every task**: \"Deploy behind VPN first; defer public access until security review.\"\n4. **Senior roles do double-checks**: Verification tasks, not implementation.\n5. **PM advice = exit criteria + risk ownership**: \"Exit criteria: 3 pilots running, dashboard live, feedback collected.\"\n6. **Testable tasks**: Each task has a clear \"done\" state someone else can verify.\n7. **Reference real systems**: ServerM1, Proxmox, GPT-5.4, Next.js, Vercel, specific tools.\n8. **No vague scope**: Not \"make it better\" but \"Benchmark 8B Q4 and 14B Q4 on ServerM1.\"\n9. **Include what NOT to build**: Explicitly defer out-of-scope work to future sprints.\n\n## Jira integration\n\nTasks can be pushed to Jira via Zapier MCP:\n- Action: `jira_software_cloud_create_issue`\n- Issue type: \"Task\" for action items, \"Story\" for features\n- Summary: Task text\n- Description: Business model slug, sprint number, role, and full context\n\n## Reference document\n\nThe canonical sprint format reference is at:\n`business_idea_generator/docs/sprint-action-reference.md`\n\nAnd the live example sprint is at:\n`business_idea_generator/docs/sprint-planning-forum-vc.md`\n\n## Pitfalls\n\n- Do not write tasks that are too abstract — \"research the market\" is not a sprint task\n- Do not skip double-checks — senior review prevents quality issues\n- Do not let scope creep — explicitly list what NOT to build\n- Do not forget exit criteria — the PM must define when the sprint is done\n- Do not assign tasks without considering the team's actual capabilities (SaaS team = software + AI, no physical products)\n", "path": "entrepreneurship/jira-sprint-task-writing/SKILL.md", "skill_dir": "/home/jordatech/.hermes/profiles/agent_stea004_entrepreneur/skills/entrepreneurship/jira-sprint-task-writing", "linked_files": null, "usage_hint": null, "required_environment_variables": [], "required_commands": [], "missing_required_environment_variables": [], "missing_credential_files": [], {"output": "#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"PVE API helper - authenticates via LLDAP bot account (creds staged in 600 file).\nNever prints credentials. Usage: pve_api.py <method> <path> [json_body]\nSpecial commands: login, permissions, realms, nodes, resources,\nvmconfig <node> <vmid>, vmstatus <node> <vmid>, raw <METHOD> <path> [json]\"\"\"\nimport sys, json, ssl, urllib.request, urllib.parse, os\n\nBASE = \"https://10.0.20.100:8006\"\nCTX = ssl.create_default_context()\nCTX.check_hostname = False\nCTX.verify_mode = ssl.CERT_NONE\nCREDS = os.path.expanduser(\"~/.pve_ldap_bot\")\n\n\ndef load_creds():\n    with open(CREDS) as f:\n        lines = [l.rstrip(\"\\n\") for l in f if l.strip()]\n    return lines[0], lines[1]\n\n\ndef login():\n    user, pw = load_creds()\n    data = urllib.parse.urlencode({\"username\": user + \"@LLDAP-Domain\", \"password\": pw}).encode()\n    req = urllib.request.Request(BASE + \"/api2/json/access/ticket\", data=data)\n    with urllib.request.urlopen(req, context=CTX, timeout=10) as r:\n        d = json.load(r)[\"data\"]\n    return d[\"ticket\"], d[\"CSRFPreventionToken\"], user + \"@LLDAP-Domain\"\n\n\ndef api(method, path, body=None):\n===\n/home/jordatech/.pve_ldap_bot\npve-creds-ok", "exit_code": 0, "error": null}

)\n114|\n115|\n116|def clear_session_cookie(response, secure: bool) -> None:\n117|    response.delete_cookie(\n118|        COOKIE_NAME,\n119|        path=\"/\",\n120|        secure=secure,\n121| # AGENTS.md

Instructions for autonomous and semi-autonomous agents working in ACMS.

## Authority model

Humans set product intent, approve scope, approve significant decisions, define mandatory governance boundaries, and approve merges.

Agents may inspect the repository, plan, implement approved requirements, decompose approved scope into tasks, create tests, update affected documentation, create handoffs, add out-of-scope discoveries to `FUTURE_WORK.md`, draft ADRs/TDRs, and execute non-destructive work inside explicitly approved scope/budget/security/environment boundaries.

Agents must not silently add/remove product scope, promote future work, accept their own new significant ADR, merge their own feature work, exceed a budget, cross tenant/context boundaries, deploy to production, alter credentials/permissions, perform destructive infrastructure actions, or modify protected Organization/Product/Project/Feature context without required human authorization.

## Read before planning

For every meaningful change read:

1. `README.md`
2. `REQUIREMENTS.md`
3. `SPRINT.md`
4. `docs/BUSINESS_PROBLEMS.md`
5. `docs/ARCHITECTURE.md`
6. relevant accepted/proposed ADRs
7. `FUTURE_WORK.md` when scope/follow-up work is involved
8. `INITIAL_IDEAS.md` when source provenance matters

## Requirement discipline

Implementation must trace to one or more `ACMS-REQ-###` entries. Before editing summarize the requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, files/components likely to change, assumptions, and validation.

If work is outside approved requirements, ask for human approval or record it in `FUTURE_WORK.md`.

## Future-work provenance

Every future-work item must identify one of:

- **Human-Directed**
- **Agent-Discovered**
- **Source-Derived**

Human-Directed does not mean approved; it remains future scope until promoted.

## Centralized governance, decentralized execution

ACMS controls approved assignment, scope, budget, context access, permissions, and governance. It must not become the synchronous permission service for every shell command/tool call/test/retry.

A worker agent may autonomously execute implementation details inside its approved envelope.

An agent may have:

- one primary ACMS work assignment; and
- zero or more registered background routines/cron jobs.

## Context authority

Protected context levels:

- Organization
- Product
- Project
- Feature

Agents may propose changes but may not modify these without human authorization or explicit delegated authority.

Agent, Task, and Live Session context may be modified within approved policy.

If approved scope appears wrong, execute it as far as reasonably possible, preserve state, create a handoff, and propose the alternate direction instead of silently changing scope.

## Handoffs

Meaningful work must produce/update a Markdown handoff recording:

1. assigned goal and requirement IDs;
2. what was attempted;
3. what actually changed;
4. what the agent believes the current state is;
5. validation and results;
6. blockers/assumptions/unresolved questions;
7. recommended next action.

## ADR policy

Human-explicit durable architecture decisions may be recorded as `Accepted`. Agent-originated significant decisions remain `Proposed` until human approval.

## Approval timeout policy

- Reversible in-scope decision: checkpoint/preserve state, use documented fallback, continue if safe.
- Consequential but deferable action: defer that action and continue unrelated work.
- Mandatory human gate: never infer approval from timeout; hold/deny the gated action and continue only unrelated authorized work.

## Security

- Never commit credentials/secrets/customer-sensitive data.
- External/customer agents are a separate trust domain.
- Internal Agent Bridge endpoints are private-network/VPN endpoints unless an accepted ADR says otherwise.
- Do not weaken authentication/authorization/audit to simplify implementation.

## Git workflow

The documentation bootstrap is human-authorized for direct import. After bootstrap, feature work must use a dedicated branch and PR.

Recommended branch: `type/ACMS-short-description`.

Recommended commit: `type(scope): short description (ACMS-REQ-###)` plus issue/ticket when available.

Every feature PR must list requirement IDs, acceptance criteria, validation, risks, ADR/TDR changes, future work, and reviewer focus. Agents do not self-merge.

the dispatch path preserves the fact).\n15|- Idempotency: a client-supplied idempotency key (or a stable hash of the\n16|  work/assignment pair) maps to ONE execution task; retries return the\n17|  original task instead of dispatching twice.\n18|- Correlation recorded on the task + event: Work ID/key, assignment, bridge\n19|  run id, budget-check verdict (audit trail), external A2A task id.\n20|\"\"\"\n21|from __future__ import annotations\n22|\n23|import hashlib\n24|import json\n25|from typing import Any\n26|\n27|from sqlalchemy import select\n28|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n29|\n30|from . import budget_service, work_service\n31|from .session_lifecycle import fail_session_for_dispatch, open_session_for_dispatch\n32|from .bridge import BridgeError, get_bridge_for_agent\n33|from .telemetry_models import AgentEventRecord\n34|from .telemetry_service import add_event\n35|from .work_models import ExecutionTaskCreate, ExecutionTaskRecord, WorkItemRecord\n36|\n37|\n38|class DispatchError(Exception):\n39|    \"\"\"Machine-readable dispatch failure (message = stable error code).\"\"\"\n40|\n41|    def __init__(self, code: str, http_status: int = 409, detail: dict | None = None):\n42|        super().__init__(code)\n43|        self.code = code\n44|        self.http_status = http_status\n45|        self.detail = detail or {}\n46|\n47|\n48|def _idempotency_key(work_item_id: str, assignment_id: str | None,\n49|                     client_key: str | None) -> str:\n50|    \"\"\"Stable dedupe key. Client key wins; else the open assignment; else work.\"\"\"\n51|    basis = client_key or assignment_id or work_item_id\n52|    return hashlib.sha256(f\"acms-dispatch:{basis}\".encode()).hexdigest()[:32]\n53|\n54|\n55|async def _find_existing_task(db: AsyncSession, dedupe_key: str) -> Any | None:\n56|    \"\"\"The most recent task created from this dedupe key.\n57|\n58|    The dedupe ledger lives in the durable EXECUTION_DISPATCHED event metadata\n59|    (``dedupe_key`` → ``task_id``) — external_task_id now carries the A2A run\n60|    id (ADR-0012 callback binding), so it can no longer double as the idem-\n61|    potency ledger. The event log is the same durability class ACMS already\n62|    relies on for reassignment counting (PR #32 pattern).\n63|    \"\"\"\n64|    rows = (await db.scalars(\n65|        select(AgentEventRecord)\n66|        .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type == \"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\")\n67|        .order_by(AgentEventRecord.timestamp.desc())\n68|        .limit(200)\n69|    )).all()\n70|    for ev in rows:  # newest first\n71|        try:\n72|            meta = json.loads(ev.metadata_json) if ev.metadata_json else {}\n73|        except (TypeError, ValueError):\n74|            continue\n75|        if meta.get(\"dedupe_key\") == dedupe_key and meta.get(\"task_id\"):\n76|            return await db.get(ExecutionTaskRecord, meta[\"task_id\"])\n77|    return None\n78|\n79|\n80|async def dispatch_work(db: AsyncSession, *, work_item_id: str,\n81|                        instruction: str, agent_id: str | None = None,\n82|                        idempotency_key: str | None = None,\n83|                        session_id: str | None = None,\n84|                        actor: str = \"api\") -> dict[str, Any]:\n85|    \"\"\"Dispatch NEW cloud execution for a Work Item — the ONLY path that does.\n86|\n87|    Returns a dict:\n88|      dispatched: bool          — False when the gate blocked or dedupe hit\n89|      reason: str               — dispatched | hard_budget_exceeded | duplicate\n90|      task_id / external_task_id — correlation ids\n91|      budget_check: dict        — the verdict used for the gate (audit trail)\n92|    Raises DispatchError for structural failures (unknown work/agent/bridge).\n93|    \"\"\"\n94|    # ---- 1. resolve the work item (+ open assignment) ----------------------\n95|    work = await db.get(WorkItemRecord, work_item_id)\n96|    if work is None:\n97|        raise DispatchError(\"unknown-work-item\", 404)\n98|    assignments = await work_service.list_assignments(db, work_item_id=work_item_id)\n99|    active = next((a for a in assignments if a.status == \"ACTIVE\"), None)\n100|    if agent_id is None and active is None:\n101|        raise DispatchError(\"no-active-assignment\", 409)\n102|    target_agent = agent_id or active.agent_id\n103|    if active is not None and agent_id is not None and agent_id != active.agent_id:\n104|        raise DispatchError(\"agent-mismatch\", 409,\n105|                            {\"active_assignment_agent\": active.agent_id})\n106|\n107|    # ---- 2. idempotency: a retry must never double-dispatch ----------------\n108|    dedupe_key = _idempotency_key(work_item_id, active.assignment_id if active else None,\n109|                                  idempotency_key)\n110|    existing = await _find_existing_task(db, dedupe_key)\n111|    if existing is not None and existing.status == \"RUNNING\":\n112|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"duplicate\",\n113|                \"task_id\": existing.task_id, \"external_task_id\": None,\n114|                \"budget_check\": None}\n115|\n116|    # ---- 2b. JIRA KICKOFF GATE (window-5 §7/§13) BEFORE budget -------------\n117|    # Server-enforced on EVERY dispatch path (UI, API, scheduler, retry,\n118|    # resume, Executive decomposition, product bootstrap — all converge here).\n119|    # Live Jira re-read immediately before the decision (§13.4 step 5).\n120|    from .jira_gate import (ELIGIBLE, JiraGateError, gate_work_item)\n121|\n122|    try:\n123|        gate = await gate_work_item(db, work_item_id, live_check=True)\n124|    except JiraGateError as e:\n125|        raise DispatchError(\"jira-gate-error\", 500, {\"detail\": str(e)[:200]}) from None\n126|    if not gate.eligible:\n127|        await add_event(\n128|            db,\n129|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n130|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n131|            agent_id=target_agent,\n132|            work_key=work.work_key,\n133|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n134|            summary=(f\"NEW execution blocked by Jira kickoff gate on \"\n135|                     f\"{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]}: {gate.verdict} — \"\n136|                     f\"{gate.reason[:300]}\")[:512],\n137|            metadata={\"gate_verdict\": gate.to_dict(), \"work_item_id\": work_item_id,\n138|                      \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"gate\": \"jira\"},\n139|        )\n140|        await db.commit()\n141|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": f\"jira_gate_{gate.verdict.lower()}\",\n142|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None,\n143|                \"budget_check\": None, \"jira_gate\": gate.to_dict()}\n144|\n145|    # ---- 3. budget gate (REQ-059) BEFORE any cloud execution ---------------\n146|    verdict = await budget_service.check_new_execution_allowed(db, work_item_id)\n147|    if not verdict.get(\"allowed\", True):\n148|        # hard exceeded without override → durable rejection event (Attention\n149|        # picks it up from the log; no separate mutable state) + hold dispatch.\n150|        await add_event(\n151|            db,\n152|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_REJECTED\",\n153|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n154|            agent_id=target_agent,\n155|            work_key=work.work_key,\n156|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n157|            summary=(\"NEW cloud execution blocked: hard budget exceeded on \"\n158|                     f\"{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]} \"\n159|                     f\"(state={verdict.get('budget_state')}; \"\n160|                     f\"override={'active' if (verdict.get('override') or {}).get('active') else 'none'})\")[:512],\n161|            metadata={\"budget_check\": verdict, \"dispatch_attempt\": True,\n162|                      \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key},\n163|        )\n164|        await db.commit()\n165|        return {\"dispatched\": False, \"reason\": \"hard_budget_exceeded\",\n166|                \"task_id\": None, \"external_task_id\": None,\n167|                \"budget_check\": verdict}\n168|\n169|    # ---- 4. resolve the bridge target --------------------------------------\n170|    bridge = get_bridge_for_agent(target_agent)\n171|    if bridge is None:\n172|        raise DispatchError(\"no-bridge-target\", 503, {\"agent_id\": target_agent})\n173|\n174|    # ---- 4b. pre-create the execution task so the runtime can call back ----\n175|    # ADR-0012: the completion callback binds task_id + agent id, so the task\n176|    # row must exist BEFORE the run starts and its id must travel with the\n177|    # instruction. Failure to record the task aborts the dispatch (nothing\n178|    # was sent to the bridge — budget not consumed, no session created).\n179|    task, err = await work_service.create_execution_task(db, ExecutionTaskCreate(\n180|        work_item_id=work_item_id,\n181|        agent_id=target_agent,\n182|        external_task_id=None,  # a2a run id back-filled after send_work\n183|    ))\n184|    if task is None:\n185|        raise DispatchError(err or \"task-record-failed\", 500)\n186|\n187|    # ADR-0012 primary completion path: the instruction carries the callback\n188|    # contract so the runtime reports its terminal result itself. The\n189|    # reconcile sweep stays the fallback for lost/late callbacks.\n190|    from .settings import get_settings\n191|\n192|    callback_base = get_settings().callback_base_url or \"\"\n193|    dispatch_instruction = instruction\n194|    if callback_base:\n195|        dispatch_instruction = (\n196|            f\"{instruction}\\n\\n---\\nCOMPLETION PROTOCOL (ACMS ADR-0012): when this task \"\n197|            f\"reaches a terminal state, POST to \"\n198|            f\"{callback_base}/api/v1/callbacks/execution-completion with header \"\n199|            f\"\\\"Authorization: Bearer <ACMS_CALLBACK_TOKEN>\\\" and JSON body \"\n200|            f'{{\"task_id\": \"{task.task_id}\", \"acms_agent_id\": \"{target_agent}\", '\n201|            f'\"status\": \"SUCCEEDED\"|\"FAILED\"|\"CANCELLED\", \"completed_at\": \"<UTC ISO>\", '\n202|            f'\"error_summary\": \"<one line when failed>\"}}. '\n203|            f\"Your agent id for acms_agent_id is {target_agent}. \"\n204|            f\"ACMS runtime infrastructure also observes completion and delivers \"\n205|            f\"the callback itself — this hint is a redundant optional path.\"\n206|        )\n207|\n208|    # ---- 5. dispatch through the bridge (new A2A run) ----------------------\n209|    session_for_run = None  # set on success; failure path closes it if set\n210|    try:\n211|        result = bridge.send_work(\n212|            work_key=work.work_key or work_item_id,\n213|            assignment_key=(active.assignment_key if active else \"\") or \"\",\n214|            instruction=dispatch_instruction,\n215|            session_id=session_id,\n216|        )\n217|    except BridgeError as e:\n218|        # dispatch itself failed — record it; budget NOT consumed (ACMS did not\n219|        # create a session), retry after the error is resolved.\n220|        await add_event(\n221|            db,\n222|            event_type=\"EXECUTION_FAILED\",\n223|            actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n224|            agent_id=target_agent,\n225|            work_key=work.work_key,\n226|            assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n227|            summary=f\"A2A dispatch failed: {str(e)[:180]}\",\n228|            metadata={\"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n229|                      \"work_item_id\": work_item_id},\n230|        )\n231|        await fail_session_for_dispatch(db, session=session_for_run, reason=str(e))\n232|        await db.commit()\n233|        raise DispatchError(\"bridge-error\", 502, {\"detail\": str(e)[:300]}) from None\n234|\n235|    run_id = (result or {}).get(\"run_id\") or (result or {}).get(\"id\")\n236|\n237|    # back-fill the A2A run id on the pre-created task (ADR-0012 binding)\n238|    if run_id:\n239|        task.external_task_id = str(run_id)\n240|\n241|    # Phase F (plan §8): the dispatch automatically owns an ExecutionSession.\n242|    # Idempotent on the A2A run id — a duplicate dispatch never duplicates it.\n243|    session_for_run = await open_session_for_dispatch(\n244|        db, agent_id=str(target_agent), work_item_id=work_item_id,\n245|        assignment_id=(active.assignment_id if active else None),\n246|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n247|        harness_session_id=(result or {}).get(\"session_id\"),\n248|    )\n249|\n250|    # ---- 6. execution task pre-created in step 4b (ADR-0012); the dedupe\n251|    # ledger is preserved on the event metadata (``disp-<key>`` remains the\n252|    # client-visible idempotency marker) ------------------------\n253|\n254|    # ---- 7. durable audit trail --------------------------------------------\n255|    await add_event(\n256|        db,\n257|        event_type=\"EXECUTION_DISPATCHED\",\n258|        actor_source=\"dispatch\",\n259|        agent_id=target_agent,\n260|        work_key=work.work_key,\n261|        assignment_key=active.assignment_key if active else None,\n262|        a2a_task_id=str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n263|        summary=(f\"Dispatched [{work.work_key or work_item_id[:8]}] to {str(target_agent)[:8]}\"\n264|                 + (f\" (soft exceeded: {verdict.get('budget_state')})\"\n265|                    if verdict.get(\"budget_state\") == \"SOFT_EXCEEDED\" else \"\"))[:512],\n266|        metadata={\"run_id\": run_id, \"budget_check\": verdict,\n267|                  \"dedupe_key\": dedupe_key, \"task_id\": task.task_id,\n268|                  \"assignment_id\": active.assignment_id if active else None,\n269|                  \"work_item_id\": work_item_id, \"actor\": actor},\n270|    )\n271|    await db.commit()\n272|\n273|    # ---- 8. runtime-driven completion watcher (ADR-0012 primary path) ------\n274|    # Infrastructure observes the bridge run and delivers the completion\n275|    # callback itself — never dependent on the model following instructions\n276|    # (window-4 plan §C1). Scheduled AFTER commit; failures never break the\n277|    # dispatch result (watch scheduling is fail-open, reconcile is fallback).\n278|    if run_id:\n279|        from .run_watcher import schedule_run_watch\n280|\n281|        schedule_run_watch(task.task_id)\n282|\n283|    return {\"dispatched\": True, \"reason\": verdict.get(\"reason\", \"dispatched\"),\n284|            \"task_id\": task.task_id, \"external_task_id\": str(run_id) if run_id else None,\n285|            \"budget_check\": verdict}\n286|", "total_lines": 285, "file_size": 14122, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

transport/protocol failure (maps to reconciliation failure).\"\"\"\n36|\n37|\n38|def _post_json(url: str, key: str, body: dict | None = None, method: str = \"POST\",\n39|               headers: dict | None = None, timeout: int = 20) -> dict:\n40|    data = json.dumps(body or {}).encode()\n41|    req = urllib.request.Request(url, data=data, method=method)\n42|    req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n43|    req.add_header(\"Content-Type\", \"application/json\")\n44|    for k, v in (headers or {}).items():\n45|        req.add_header(k, v)\n46|    try:\n47|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n48|            return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n49|    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n50|        detail = e.read().decode(errors=\"replace\")[:300]\n51|        raise BridgeError(f\"{e.code} {detail}\") from None\n52|    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n53|        raise req.add_header(\"Authorization\", f\"Bearer {key}\")\n59|    try:\n60|        with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=timeout) as resp:\n61|            return json.loads(resp.read().decode())\n62|    except urllib.error.HTTPError as e:\n63|        raise BridgeError(f\"{e.code}\") from None\n64|    except (urllib.error.URLError, TimeoutError, OSError) as e:\n65|        raise BridgeError(str(e)) from None\n66|\n67|\n68|@dataclass\n69|class BridgeTarget:\n70|    agent_id: str\n71|    base_url: str\n72|    api_key: str\n73|    harness: str = \"hermes\"\n74|\n75|\n76|def load_targets() -> list[BridgeTarget]:\n77|    from .settings import get_settings\n78|\n79|    raw = get_settings().bridge_targets_json\n80|    targets = []\n81|    for item in json.loads(raw) if raw else []:\n82|        targets.append(BridgeTarget(\n83|            agent_id=item[\"agent_id\"],\n84|            base_url=item[\"base_url\"].rstrip(\"/\"),\n85|            api_key=item[\"api_key\"],\n86|            harness=item.get(\"harness\", \"hermes\"),\n87|        ))\n88|    return targets\n89|\n90|\n91|def get_bridge_for_agent(agent_id: str):\n92|    \"\"\"Factory used by the reconciliation scheduler (lazy import kept here).\n93|\n94|    Phase G: resolution is ARM-authoritative (bridge_discovery); the manual\n95|    ACMS_BRIDGE_TARGETS_JSON list remains as fallback. Fail-closed either way.\n96|    \"\"\"\n97|    from .bridge_discovery import DiscoveryError, discover_bridge\n98|\n99|    try:\n100|        d = discover_bridge(agent_id)\n101|        return HermesBridge(BridgeTarget(\n102|            agent_id=d.agent_id, base_url=d.base_url,\n103|            api_key=d.api_key, harness=d.harness,\n104|        ))\n105|    except DiscoveryError as e:\n106|        raise BridgeError(str(e)) from None\n107|\n108|\n109|class HermesBridge:\n110|    \"\"\"Tested Hermes api-server mapping. Every method here corresponds to a\n111|    row in docs/HARNESS_CONTROL_MAPPING.md.\"\"\"\n112|\n113|    def __init__(self, target: BridgeTarget):\n114|        self._t = target\n115|\n116|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- status\n117|\n118|    def fetch_status(self) -> HeartbeatPayload:\n119|        \"\"\"Full status snapshot via the tested mapping (reconciliation path).\n120|\n121|        Raises BridgeError when unreachable — the scheduler maps that to\n122|        UNREACHABLE per ADR-0010.\"\"\"\n123|        sessions = self._get(\"/api/sessions\")\n124|        rows = sessions.get(\"data\") or sessions.get(\"sessions\") or []\n125|        if not rows:\n126|            raise BridgeError(\"no sessions reported\")\n127|        # Most recently active session = the agent's current work context.\n128|        rows.sort(key=lambda s: s.get(\"last_active\") or 0, reverse=True)\n129|        cur = rows[0]\n130|        sess = self._get(f\"/api/sessions/{cur['id']}\").get(\"session\", {})\n131|        return HeartbeatPayload(\n132|            schema_version=\"acms-heartbeat-v1\",\n133|            identity=HeartbeatIdentity(\n134|                acms_agent_id=self._t.agent_id,\n135|                bridge_id=\"hermes-api-server\",\n136|                harness=\"hermes\",\n137|            ),\n138|            session=HeartbeatSession(\n139|                session_id=str(sess.get(\"id\") or cur.get(\"id\") or \"\"),\n140|                title=sess.get(\"title\"),\n141|                agent_running=False,  # sessions API has no live running flag; reconciliation is contact-only\n142|            ),\n143|            platforms={\"connected\": [\"api_server\"]},\n144|        )\n145|\n146|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- controls\n147|\n148|    def send_work(self, *, work_key: str, assignment_key: str, instruction: str,\n149|                  session_id: str | None = None) -> dict:\n150|        \"\"\"Dispatch work. Uses run submission (new session) or session-bound\n151|        chat (existing session); includes ACMS keys in metadata per REQ-052.\"\"\"\n152|        meta = {\"acms_work_key\": work_key, \"acms_assignment_key\": assignment_key,\n153|                \"source\": \"acms\"}\n154|        if session_id:\n155|            return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n156|                              self._t.api_key,\n157|                              {\"message\": instruction},\n158|                              headers={\"X-Hermes-Session-Id\": session_id})\n159|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/v1/runs\", self._t.api_key, {\n160|            \"input\": [{\"role\": \"user\", \"content\": instruction}],\n161|            \"metadata\": meta,\n162|            \"title\": f\"[{work_key}] ACMS dispatched work\",\n163|        })\n164|\n165|    def steer(self, session_id: str, message: str) -> dict:\n166|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}/chat\",\n167|                          self._t.api_key,\n168|                          {\"message\": message},\n169|                          headers={\"X-Hermes-Session-Id\": session_id})\n170|\n171|    def interrupt(self, run_id: str) -> dict:\n172|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/v1/runs/{run_id}/stop\", self._t.api_key)\n173|\n174|    # `cancel` uses the same tested terminal endpoint as interrupt (A2A-task\n175|    # level); the Hermes session survives — new execution task to continue.\n176|    cancel = interrupt\n177|\n178|    def set_session_title(self, session_id: str, title: str) -> dict:\n179|        return _post_json(f\"{self._t.base_url}/api/sessions/{session_id}\",\n180|                          self._t.api_key, {\"title\": title}, method=\"PATCH\")\n181|\n182|    def request_handoff(self, session_id: str, work_key: str) -> dict:\n183|        \"\"\"Plan §10: structured Markdown handoff request (via send_work).\"\"\"\n184|        instruction = (\n185|            f\"Produce a structured Markdown handoff for [{work_key}]: what you \"\n186|            \"believe you are doing, current goal, actions completed, \"\n187|            \"artifacts/files changed, blockers, and next intended action. \"\n188|            \"Use 'Observed:' vs 'Interpretation:' conventions.\"\n189|        )\n190|        return self.send_work(work_key=work_key, assignment_key=\"\",\n191|                              instruction=instruction, session_id=session_id)\n192|\n193|    # ---------------------------------------------------------------- helpers\n194|\n195|    def _get(self, path: str) -> dict:\n196|        return _get_json(self._t.base_url, self._t.api_key, path)\n197|", "total_lines": 196, "file_size": 8215, "truncated": false, "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

### [2026-10-01T01:38:15.259276+00:00] assistant

MIAM-00100 has `local-lvm` (1.8TB, 2% used) and `local` (81GB free) — plenty for worker disks. VM124's disk is on `testthin` (miam00111-local), so **cross-node migration requires disk move or recreation from template**. VM124 is 200G — local-lvm on MIAM-00100 can hold it. Options: (a) `qm move-disk` won't work cross-node directly; (b) storage migration via `qm migrate --with-local-disks` (slow but works for 200G thin-provisioned); (c) recreate from template VM 121. Let me check what template 121 is and whether it's on shared storage.

{"status": "success", "output": "highest REQs: ACMS-REQ-058\nACMS-REQ-059\nACMS-REQ-060\nACMS-REQ-061\nACMS-REQ-062\n\n# ADR-0010: Agent heartbeat, session/work correlation, reconciliation, and A2A live control\n\n**Status:** Accepted (human-approved defaults, combined slice 3+4 plan 2026-09-26 §2.2)\n**Date:** 2026-09-26\n\n## Context\n\nACMS currently records management state (Work Items, primary assignments,\nexecution tasks, routines) but has no live view of agent connectivity, running\nstate, session identity, or context telemetry, and no way to deliver work or\nsteer agents (REQ-031/033/034/035/036/037 remain partially implemented). The\ncombined slice 3+4 plan (2026-09-26) directs ACMS to become a real workforce\ncontrol plane. This ADR records the human-approved defaults and invariants for\nthat slice.\n\n## Decision\n\n### Approved operational defaults (configuration-backed, not UI-controlled in MVP)\n\n```text\nheartbeat interval               60 seconds   (ACMS_HEARTBEAT_INTERVAL_SECONDS)\nSTALE threshold                 300 seconds   (ACMS_HEARTBEAT_STALE_SECONDS)\nstale reconciliation threshold 3600 seconds   (ACMS_STALE_RECONCILE_SECONDS)\nfleet-wide reconciliation      86400 seconds   (ACMS_FLEET_RECONCILE_SECONDS)\nsession-alignment grace        120 seconds   (ACMS_SESSION_ALIGNMENT_GRACE_SECONDS)\ncontext ELEVATED                70 percent   (ACMS_CONTEXT_ELEVATED_PERCENT)\ncontext HIGH                    85 percent   (ACMS_CONTEXT_HIGH_PERCENT)\ncontext CRITICAL                95 percent   (ACMS_CONTEXT_CRITICAL_PERCENT)\ntelemetry sample interval      300 seconds   (ACMS_TELEMETRY_SAMPLE_SECONDS)\n```\n\n### State-dimension invariants (plan §5)\n\n1. **Connectivity** is derived by ACMS from contact recency: `UNKNOWN` (never\n   contacted), `HEALTHY` (recent heartbeat/reconciliation), `STALE` (> 5 min\n   without contact), `UNREACHABLE` (stale ≥ 1 h AND active reconciliation\n   failed). `last_contact_source` records how (heartbeat / reconciliation /\n   registration).\n2. **`agent_running`** is harness-reported tri-state (`true` / `false` /\n   `unknown`): whether the harness has an active agent execution — NOT whether\n   the VM/gateway is reachable. `Connectivity: HEALTHY` + `Agent Running: No`\n   is a valid, normal combination.\n3. **A2A task state** uses the A2A protocol's native task-state model\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Background scheduling for live telemetry (combined slice 3+4; ADR-0010; plan §16).\n2|\n3|Lightweight in-process loop — no Celery/K8s/event bus. Runs inside the FastAPI\n4|lifespan; every DB operation gets its own session so the loop never holds a\n5|request-scoped session. Time is injected (``now_fn``) so tests never wait real\n6|minutes/hours (plan §20).\n7|\n8|Jobs:\n9|- stale evaluation (every tick): derive UNKNOWN/HEALTHY/STALE/UNREACHABLE\n10|- 1-hour stale reconciliation: actively request a full status snapshot\n11|- 24 h fleet reconciliation (staggered tolerance documented in ADR-0010)\n12|- telemetry sampling every ACMS_TELEMETRY_SAMPLE_SECONDS + on material change\n13|\n14|Reconciliation authority (plan §9): correct operational telemetry, preserve\n15|history, log discrepancy events; never touch approved scope/assignment.\n16|Multi-replica note: a single ACMS instance is assumed; replicas would need\n17|leader/lock coordination (documented in ADR-0010 + FUTURE_WORK).\n18|\"\"\"\n19|from __future__ import annotations\n20|\n21|import asyncio\n22|import json\n23|import logging\n24|from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone\n25|\n26|from sqlalchemy import select\n27|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n28|\n29|from .db import SessionLocal\n30|from .telemetry_models import (\n31|    AgentEventRecord,\n32|    AgentStatusCurrentRecord,\n33|    AgentTelemetrySampleRecord,\n34|    CONNECTIVITY_HEALTHY,\n35|    CONNECTIVITY_STALE,\n36|    CONNECTIVITY_UNREACHABLE,\n37|    CONTACT_SOURCE_RECONCILIATION,\n38|)\n39|from .telemetry_service import TelemetryService, _new_id, add_event, _now\n40|from .settings import get_settings\n41|\n42|logger = logging.getLogger(\"acms.telemetry.scheduler\")\n43|\n44|\n45|class TelemetryScheduler:\n46|    def __init__(self, session_factory=None, now_fn=None):\n47|        self._session_factory = session_factory or SessionLocal\n48|        self._now_fn = now_fn or _now\n49|        self._task: asyncio.Task | None = None\n50|        self._last_fleet_reconcile: datetime | None = None\n51|        self._last_result_reconcile: datetime | None = None\n52|        self._last_sample: dict[str, datetime] = {}\n53|        # C4 state-entry dedupe: AGENT_RUNNING_WITHOUT_ASSIGNMENT is emitted once\n54|        # per state entry (never per tick). Key = agent_id, value = currently in state.\n55|        self._running_unassigned: dict[str, bool] = {}\n56|\n57|    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ loop\n58|\n59|    def start(self) -> None:\n60|        if self._task is None or self._task.done():\n61|            self._task = asyncio.create_task(self._run(), name=\"acms-telemetry-scheduler\")\n62|    async def stop(self) -> None:\n63|        if self._task is not None:\n64|            self._task.cancel()\n65|            try:\n66|                await self._task\n67|            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n68|                pass\n69|            self._task = None\n70|\n71|    async def _run(self) -> None:\n72|        from .settings import get_settings\n73|\n74|        while True:\n75|            try:\n76|                s = get_settings()\n77|                await self.tick(s)\n78|            except asyncio.CancelledError:\n79|                raise\n80|            except Exception:  # noqa: BLE001 — scheduler must survive transient DB issues\n81|", "total_lines": 347, "file_size": 16395, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=81 to continue reading (showing 1-80 of 347 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

I have the full picture now. Key live facts discovered:

- **Worker Hermes 0.19.0 api-server** exposes `/v1/runs/{id}/events` SSE (`run.started/completed/failed/cancelled`, `tool.started/completed/failed`, `message.delta`, `reasoning.available`) + `/health/detailed` + `/v1/capabilities` — the live-stream plumbing exists worker-side
- `/api/sessions` rows carry token counts + cost fields — heartbeat payload source
- `fast` alias on the worker currently resolves to **qwen3.6-35b-a3b** (VM401), not flash-next
- VM114 **already** has Emporia creds (`/etc/llm-manager/secrets/emporia_creds`, 0600) + a live Emporia collector with 57k samples + channel map (ch1-3 = PDU feeds, ch4 = mini-split) — Phase 11 is a display/attribution job, not a new collector
- MIAM-00100 has `local-lvm` 1.8TB (2% used) — VM124's disk is on miam00111-local `testthin`, so migration = disk move or recreate (plan §22 anticipates both)

Now setting up the dev environment and verifying the test suite is green before implementing.

{"status": "success", "output": "{\"exitcode\": 0, \"out\": \"    def _parse_model_routes(raw: Any) -> Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]]:\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"Validate and normalize the ``model_routes`` config block.\\n\\n        Accepts a mapping of ``alias -> {model, provider?, api_key?, base_url?}``.\\n        Invalid shapes are dropped (never raised) so a config typo can't take\\n        the whole API server down.  Route values are coerced to strings.\\n\\n        Security: per-route ``api_key`` values are UPSTREAM provider\\n        credentials (used to call the routed model's backend), not caller\\n        authentication \\u00e2\\u0080\\u0094 callers still authenticate with the global\\n        API_SERVER_KEY bearer token via ``_check_auth``.  Route api_keys must\\n        never be logged; only alias names and non-secret fields may appear in\\n        logs.\\n        \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n        if not isinstance(raw, dict):\\n            if raw:\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    \\\"api_server model_routes ignored: expected a mapping, got %s\\\",\\n                    type(raw).__name__,\\n                )\\n            return {}\\n\\n        allowed_keys = (\\\"model\\\", \\\"provider\\\", \\\"api_key\\\", \\\"base_url\\\")\\n        routes: Dict[str, Dict[str, Any]] = {}\\n        for alias, cfg in raw.items():\\n            alias_str = str(alias).strip()\\n            if not alias_str or not isinstance(cfg, dict):\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    \\\"api_server model_routes: dropping invalid route entry %r\\\", alias_str or alias\\n                )\\n                continue\\n            route = {\\n                key: str(cfg[key]).strip()\\n                for key in allowed_keys\\n                if cfg.get(key) is not None and str(cfg[key]).strip()\\n            }\\n            if not route.get(\\\"model\\\"):\\n                logger.warning(\\n                    \\\"api_server model_routes: route %r has no 'model'; dropping\\\", alias_str\\n                )\\n                continue\\n            routes[alias_str] = route\\n        return routes\\n\\n    def _resolve_route(self, model_alias: Any) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:\\n\", \"err\": \"\"}\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 1.53}

{"content": "1|\"\"\"Immutable human-readable work/assignment keys (ACMS-REQ-052; ADR-0010).\n2|\n3|Allocation is a durable, transactional counter — never a row count — so keys\n4|survive deletes and concurrent transactions. Backed by the ``acms_key_counters``\n5|table (portable across SQLite and PostgreSQL; a PostgreSQL sequence would also\n6|satisfy the requirement but the counter table keeps the unit-test path and the\n7|production path identical).\n8|\n9|Format: ``ACMS-WORK-000001`` / ``ACMS-ASG-000001``. Keys are never reused:\n10|the counter only ever increments, even when rows are deleted.\n11|\"\"\"\n12|from __future__ import annotations\n13|\n14|from sqlalchemy import String, text\n15|from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession\n16|from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column\n17|\n18|from .db import Base\n19|\n20|WORK_PREFIX = \"ACMS-WORK-\"\n21|ASG_PREFIX = \"ACMS-ASG-\"\n22|\n23|\n24|async def _next_counter(db: AsyncSession, name: str) -> int:\n25|    \"\"\"Atomically increment and return the counter for ``name``.\"\"\"\n26|    await db.execute(\n27|        text(\n28|            \"INSERT INTO acms_key_counters (counter_name, counter_value) \"\n29|            \"VALUES (:name, 0) ON CONFLICT (counter_name) DO NOTHING\"\n30|        ),\n31|        {\"name\": name},\n32|    )\n33|    row = await db.execute(\n34|        text(\n35|            \"UPDATE acms_key_counters SET counter_value = counter_value + 1 \"\n36|            \"WHERE counter_name = :name RETURNING counter_value\"\n37|        ),\n38|        {\"name\": name},\n39|    )\n40|    value = row.scalar_one()\n41|    return int(value)\n42|\n43|\n44|async def allocate_work_key(db: AsyncSession) -> str:\n45|    n = await _next_counter(db, \"work_key\")\n46|", "total_lines": 71, "file_size": 2356, "truncated": true, "hint": "Use offset=46 to continue reading (showing 1-45 of 71 lines)", "is_binary": false, "is_image": false}

{"status": "success", "output": "class AttentionItem(BaseModel):\n    event_id: str\n    sequence: int\n    timestamp: str | None\n    event_type: str\n    severity: str\n    agent_id: str | None\n    summary: str\n    acknowledged: bool = False\n\n\ndef _derive(r: AgentEventRecord, severity: str | None):\n    \"\"\"Map one durable event row to an Attention dict (shared API+UI).\"\"\"\n    sev = SEVERITY.get(r.event_type, \"medium\")\n    if r.event_type == \"BUDGET_THRESHOLD_CROSSED\" and \"HARD_EXCEEDED\" in (r.summary or \"\"):\n        sev = \"critical\"\n    if r.event_type == \"EXECUTION_COMPLETED\":\n        return None  # success never surfaces as Attention (ADR-0012 policy)\n    if severity and sev != severity:\n        return None\n    return {\n        \"event_id\": r.event_id,\n        \"sequence\": r.sequence,\n        \"timestamp\": r.timestamp.isoformat() if r.timestamp else None,\n        \"timestamp_display\": r.timestamp.strftime(\"%m-%d %H:%M\") if r.timestamp else \"—\",\n        \"event_type\": r.event_type,\n        \"severity\": sev,\n        \"agent_id\": r.agent_id,\n        \"work_key\": r.work_key,\n        \"assignment_key\": r.assignment_key,\n        \"summary\": r.summary,\n    }\n\n\nasync def attention_items(db: AsyncSession, *, hours: int = DEFAULT_LOOKBACK_HOURS,\n                          severity: str | None = None) -> list[dict]:\n    \"\"\"Derived Attention feed as plain dicts — one derivation shared by the\n    JSON API, the UI page, and any future consumer (single source of truth).\"\"\"\n    since = datetime.now(timezone.utc) - timedelta(hours=hours)\n    stmt = (\n        select(AgentEventRecord)\n        .where(AgentEventRecord.event_type.in_(ATTENTION_EVENT_TYPES))\n        .where(AgentEventRecord.timestamp >= since)\n        .order_by(AgentEventRecord.sequence.desc())\n        .limit(200)\n    )\n    rows = (await db.execute(stmt)).scalars().all()\n    items = []\n    for r in rows:\n        d = _derive(r, severity)\n        if d is not None:\n            items.append(d)\n    return items\n\n\n@router.get(\"\", response_model=list[AttentionItem])\n\n", "tool_calls_made": 0, "duration_seconds": 0.02}

reuse it\n    r = client.get(\"/api/v1/agents\", headers=AUTH)\n    if r.status_code == 200 and isinstance(r.json(), list) and r.json():\n        return r.json()[0][\"agent_id\"]\n    return _mk_agent(client)\n\n\ndef _mk_work_with_assignment(client) -> str:\n    agent_id = _mk_agent(client)\n    w = client.post(\"/api/v1/work/items\", headers=AUTH,\n                    json={\"kind\": \"task\", \"title\": \"dispatch-gate\"}).json()[\"work_item_id\"]\n    # window-5 §7: link the Jira issue (real endpoint;
